Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The proofs operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 177. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 88 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to proofs theory, we decompose the problem into 177 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 88. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
